PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Chikkatekalavatti Village of
Hosadurga Taluk, Chitradurga District and to constitute a new revenue village
called Keremundalahatti and RamadasanaykanaTandya as specified in schedule
along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or

suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chitradurga
District.

SCHEDULE – 1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Chitradurga District Hosadurga Taluk to form a new revenue village
called Keremundalahatti Village of Hosadurga Taluk, Chitradurga District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 16 GLH 2023 Dated: 25-01-2023
regarding altering the limits of Paragodu village of Bagepalli Taluk and
Chikkaballapur District and constituting a new revenue villages called
Yandraganahalli was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land

Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. Part
IV-A Dated: 01-02-2023 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely
to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Chikkaballapur district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 15-02-2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Paragodu of Bagepalli taluk in Chikkaballapur District to form a new
revenue village called as Yandraganahalli of Bagepalli taluk Chikkaballapur
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS/90/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1337537 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 12-03-2024
PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Doddasiddavvanahalli
Village of Chitradurga taluk Chitradurga District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called Kyadhigere, Ennegere, Thopuramalige and kasavanahalli as
specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned
to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chitradurga
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
Doddasiddavvanahalli Village of Chitradurga taluk Chitradurga District to form a
new revenue village called Kyadhigere Village of Chitradurga taluk Chitradurga
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-667
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RC-CFS/81/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1332864 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date:18-03-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the, Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Parasurampura Village of
Challakere taluk Chitradurga District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called Hullikatte as specified in schedule.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Parasurampura village for conversion of Hullikatte unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/part of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Chitradurga District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Hullikatte village of Challakere
Taluk of Chitradurga District convert the same to form hamlet within the boundary
of Parasurampura Revenue village and same will herein after be named as called
Hullikatte of Parasurampura village in Challakere Taluk of Chitradurga
District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above
with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
located is proposed to be converted as habitation area( Gramatana) of Hullikatte
hamlet of Parasurampura village in Challakere Taluk of Chitradurga District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as
the State Government may specify by Notification issued under section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 ( Karnataka Act No. 12 of 1964 )

Governor of Karnataka

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

E-1308594 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date:07-03-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Byadarahalli Village of Hiriyuru
Taluk, Chitradurga District and to constitute a new revenue village called
R.S.Upparahatti & Haleainahalli as specified in schedule along with maps with
old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chitradurga
District.

SCHEDULE – 1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Chitradurga District Hiriyur Taluk to form a new revenue village called
R.S.Upparahatti Village of Hiriyur Taluk, Chitradurga District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-669
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RC-CFS0ADMN/91/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1292951 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560 001,
Date:02-03-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 1001 Kamgrara 2017 Dated: 11-
01-2018 regarding altering the limits of Heggere village of Hosadurga Taluk and

Chitradurga District and constituting a new revenue village called Dalavayikatte
and Obalapura was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette Part IV-A
dated: 26-04-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Chitradurga district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 17-11-2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Heggere Village of Hosadurga Taluk in Chitradurga District to form a new
revenue village called as Dalavayikatte of Hosadurga Taluk and Chitradurga
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RC-CFS/25/2023 –RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1276768 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru-560 001,
Date:11-03-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 08 GLH 2022 Dated: 19/01/2022
regarding altering the limits of Cholashettihalli village of Gowribidanuru Taluk and
Chikkaballapur District and constituting a new revenue villages called
Nadumithanda was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. 231
Part IV-A Dated: 21/04/2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons
likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the
Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Chikkaballapur district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 21/04/2022 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Cholashettihalli of Gowribidanuru taluk in Chikkaballapur District to form
a new revenue village called as Nadumithanda of Gowribidanuru taluk
Chikkaballapur District along with the survey numbers included in column (4)
thereof.

PR-671

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RC- CFS/2/2023-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1266339 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date:06-03-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kashipura Village of Channagiri
Taluk, Davangere District and to constitute a new revenue village called
Sathyanarayanapura as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Davangere
District.

SCHEDULE – 1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Davangere District Channagiri Taluk to form a new revenue village called
Sathyanarayanapura Village of Channagiri Taluk, Davangere District along with
the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Tellanuru Village of Kollegala

with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chamarajnagar
District.

Date:06-03-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No 820 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated:23-11-2017 regarding altering the limits of Kunthuru village of kollegala Taluk
and Chamarajanagara District and constituting a new revenue village called
Kunthuru Mole was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD
820 KAMGRARA 2017 dated:04-01-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from
all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner
Chamarajanagara district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 08/01/2018 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Kunthuru of Kollegala Taluk in Chamarajanagara District to form a new
revenue village called as Kunthuru Mole of Kollegala taluk Chamarajanagara
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereo

Date:28-02-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 785 KAMGRARA2017,
BANGALURU Dated 23-11-2017 regarding altering the limits of TERAMBALLI
village of Kollegala Taluk and Chamarajnagar District and constituting a new revenue
village called GOBBALLIPURA was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No. 2194 Part-IV-A dated:04-01-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from
all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner
Chamarajnagar district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 10/07/2019 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
Teramballi Village of Kollegala Taluk, Chamarajnagar district to form a new revenue
village called as Gobballipura of Kollegala Taluk and Chamarajnagar District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Chatnihalli village of
HARAPANAHALLI Taluk VIJAYANAGARA District and to constitute a new
revenue villages called Kuremaganhalli, PaniyaPura, Kenchapura, Ramnagara,

U.Kalhalli as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers
and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Vijayanagara
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
Chatnihalli village of HARAPANAHALLI Taluk VIJAYANAGARA District to
form a new revenue village called Kuremaganhalli Village of HARAPANAHALLI
Taluk VIJAYANAGARA District along with the survey numbers included in column
(4) thereof.
